| 声明 | 行为与约束 |

| <a id="api-3c411136dd61dfd9"></a>[`public ushort Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.GetGamepadProductVersionForId(uint instance_id)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L15237) | Get the product version of a gamepad, if available. This can be called before any gamepads are opened. If the product version isn't available this function returns 0. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetGamepadProductVersion @sa SDL_GetGamepads<br>`instance_id`：the joystick instance ID.<br>返回：the product version of the selected gamepad. If called on an invalid index, this function returns zero. |
| <a id="api-dda211e95268b11f"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGamepadType Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.GetGamepadTypeForId(uint instance_id)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L16276) | Get the type of a gamepad. This can be called before any gamepads are opened. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetGamepadType @sa SDL_GetGamepads @sa SDL_GetRealGamepadTypeForID<br>`instance_id`：the joystick instance ID.<br>返回：the gamepad type. |
| <a id="api-5a04fc94d368f0e7"></a>[`public ushort Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.GetGamepadVendorForId(uint instance_id)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L11488) | Get the USB vendor ID of a gamepad, if available. This can be called before any gamepads are opened. If the vendor ID isn't available this function returns 0. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetGamepadVendor @sa SDL_GetGamepads<br>`instance_id`：the joystick instance ID.<br>返回：the USB vendor ID of the selected gamepad. If called on an invalid index, this function returns zero. |
| <a id="api-fa0b92d80fa06af5"></a>[`public uint Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.GetGlobalProperties()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L12563) | Get the global SDL properties. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>返回：a valid property ID on success or 0 on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-259af913d6cd39f1"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGPUTextureFormat Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.GetGpuTextureFormatFromPixelFormat(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLPixelFormat format)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L13093) | Get the GPU texture format corresponding to an SDL pixel format. @since This function is available since SDL 3.4.0.<br>`format`：a pixel format.<br>返回：the corresponding GPU texture format, or SDL_GPU_TEXTUREFORMAT_INVALID if there is no corresponding GPU texture format. |
| <a id="api-d2bf983bc4843ddc"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGUID Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.GetJoystickGuidForId(uint instance_id)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L13410) | Get the implementation-dependent GUID of a joystick. This can be called before any joysticks are opened. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetJoystickGUID @sa SDL_GUIDToString<br>`instance_id`：the joystick instance ID.<br>返回：the GUID of the selected joystick. If called with an invalid instance_id, this function returns a zero GUID. |
| <a id="api-4b49bdb6808f55f5"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.GetJoystickPlayerIndexForId(uint instance_id)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L12688) | Get the player index of a joystick. This can be called before any joysticks are opened. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetJoystickPlayerIndex @sa SDL_GetJoysticks<br>`instance_id`：the joystick instance ID.<br>返回：the player index of a joystick, or -1 if it's not available. |
| <a id="api-773067a5c03d1dcc"></a>[`public ushort Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.GetJoystickProductForId(uint instance_id)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L12229) | Get the USB product ID of a joystick, if available. This can be called before any joysticks are opened. If the product ID isn't available this function returns 0. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetJoystickProduct @sa SDL_GetJoysticks<br>`instance_id`：the joystick instance ID.<br>返回：the USB product ID of the selected joystick. If called with an invalid instance_id, this function returns 0. |
| <a id="api-e18498288909ef9a"></a>[`public ushort Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.GetJoystickProductVersionForId(uint instance_id)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L12446) | Get the product version of a joystick, if available. This can be called before any joysticks are opened. If the product version isn't available this function returns 0. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetJoystickProductVersion @sa SDL_GetJoysticks<br>`instance_id`：the joystick instance ID.<br>返回：the product version of the selected joystick. If called with an invalid instance_id, this function returns 0. |
| <a id="api-c29fb62830b68a8a"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLJoystickType Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.GetJoystickTypeForId(uint instance_id)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L15566) | Get the type of a joystick, if available. This can be called before any joysticks are opened. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetJoystickType @sa SDL_GetJoysticks<br>`instance_id`：the joystick instance ID.<br>返回：the SDL_JoystickType of the selected joystick. If called with an invalid instance_id, this function returns `SDL_JOYSTICK_TYPE_UNKNOWN`. |
| <a id="api-ea63b4a56101c56f"></a>[`public ushort Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.GetJoystickVendorForId(uint instance_id)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L15607) | Get the USB vendor ID of a joystick, if available. This can be called before any joysticks are opened. If the vendor ID isn't available this function returns 0. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetJoystickVendor @sa SDL_GetJoysticks<br>`instance_id`：the joystick instance ID.<br>返回：the USB vendor ID of the selected joystick. If called with an invalid instance_id, this function returns 0. |
| <a id="api-7d1092cfab0d5a6a"></a>[`public uint Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.GetKeyFromScancode(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLScancode scancode, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLKeymod modstate, bool key_event)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L12425) | Get the key code corresponding to the given scancode according to the current keyboard layout. If you want to get the keycode as it would be delivered in key events, including options specified in SDL_HINT_KEYCODE_OPTIONS, then you should pass `key_event` as true. Otherwise this function simply translates the scancode based on the given modifier state. @threadsafety This function is not thread safe. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetKeyName @sa SDL_GetScancodeFromKey<br>`scancode`：the desired SDL_Scancode to query.<br>`modstate`：the modifier state to use when translating the scancode to a keycode.<br>`key_event`：true if the keycode will be used in key events.<br>返回：the SDL_Keycode that corresponds to the given SDL_Scancode. |
| <a id="api-034ab619a3ebddfc"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLLogPriority Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.GetLogPriority(int category)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L12131) | Get the priority of a particular log category. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetLogPriority<br>`category`：the category to query.<br>返回：the SDL_LogPriority for the requested category. |
| <a id="api-249b58441b6b71c9"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLKeymod Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.GetModState()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L13484) | Get the current key modifier state for the keyboard. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetKeyboardState @sa SDL_SetModState<br>返回：an OR'd combination of the modifier keys for the keyboard. |
| <a id="api-8a5c52624e1a0ca9"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLDisplayOrientation Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.GetNaturalDisplayOrientation(uint displayID)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L14331) | Get the orientation of a display when it is unrotated. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetDisplays<br>`displayID`：the instance ID of the display to query.<br>返回：the SDL_DisplayOrientation enum value of the display, or `SDL_ORIENTATION_UNKNOWN` if it isn't available. |
| <a id="api-e2379017f326c773"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.GetNumAllocations()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L12395) | Get the number of outstanding (unfreed) allocations. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>返回：the number of allocations or -1 if allocation counting is disabled. |
| <a id="api-6c6d566ba39877b8"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.GetNumAudioDrivers()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L15118) | Use this function to get the number of built-in audio drivers. This function returns a hardcoded number. This never returns a negative value; if there are no drivers compiled into this build of SDL, this function returns zero. The presence of a driver in this list does not mean it will function, it just means SDL is capable of interacting with that interface. For example, a build of SDL might have esound support, but if there's no esound server available, SDL's esound driver would fail if used. By default, SDL tries all drivers, in its preferred order, until one is found to be usable. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetAudioDriver<br>返回：the number of built-in audio drivers. |
| <a id="api-da3eb19197b37913"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.GetNumCameraDrivers()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L14907) | Use this function to get the number of built-in camera drivers. This function returns a hardcoded number. This never returns a negative value; if there are no drivers compiled into this build of SDL, this function returns zero. The presence of a driver in this list does not mean it will function, it just means SDL is capable of interacting with that interface. For example, a build of SDL might have v4l2 support, but if there's no kernel support available, SDL's v4l2 driver would fail if used. By default, SDL tries all drivers, in its preferred order, until one is found to be usable. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetCameraDriver<br>返回：the number of built-in camera drivers. |
| <a id="api-b419704ae2e71801"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.GetNumGpuDrivers()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L13005) | Get the number of GPU drivers compiled into SDL. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetGPUDriver<br>返回：the number of built in GPU drivers. |
| <a id="api-2090cae6c062d6b6"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.GetNumLogicalCpuCores()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L12578) | Get the number of logical CPU cores available. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>返回：the total number of logical CPU cores. On CPUs that include technologies such as hyperthreading, the number of logical cores may be more than the number of physical cores. |
| <a id="api-ecd72d8d42da1421"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.GetNumRenderDrivers()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L15512) | Get the number of 2D rendering drivers available for the current display. A render driver is a set of code that handles rendering and texture management on a particular display. Normally there is only one, but some drivers may have several available with different capabilities. There may be none if SDL was compiled without render support. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_CreateRenderer @sa SDL_GetRenderDriver<br>返回：the number of built in render drivers. |
| <a id="api-6c882f8f611f8de0"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.GetNumVideoDrivers()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L16009) | Get the number of video drivers compiled into SDL. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetVideoDriver<br>返回：the number of built in video drivers. |
| <a id="api-be5317fa1efdf596"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLPenDeviceType Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.GetPenDeviceType(uint instance_id)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L16325) | Get the device type of the given pen. Many platforms do not supply this information, so an app must always be prepared to get an SDL_PEN_DEVICE_TYPE_UNKNOWN result. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.4.0.<br>`instance_id`：the pen instance ID.<br>返回：the device type of the given pen, or SDL_PEN_DEVICE_TYPE_INVALID on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-8525b0c98928a5be"></a>[`public ulong Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.GetPerformanceCounter()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L12992) | Get the current value of the high resolution counter. This function is typically used for profiling. The counter values are only meaningful relative to each other. Differences between values can be converted to times by using SDL_GetPerformanceFrequency(). @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetPerformanceFrequency<br>返回：the current counter value. |
| <a id="api-6dfc34eca21c672f"></a>[`public ulong Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.GetPerformanceFrequency()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L14680) | Get the count per second of the high resolution counter. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetPerformanceCounter<br>返回：a platform-specific count per second. |
| <a id="api-a753b66da038aa58"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLPixelFormat Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.GetPixelFormatForMasks(int bpp, uint Rmask, uint Gmask, uint Bmask, uint Amask)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L13564) | Convert a bpp value and RGBA masks to an enumerated pixel format. This will return `SDL_PIXELFORMAT_UNKNOWN` if the conversion wasn't possible. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetMasksForPixelFormat<br>`bpp`：a bits per pixel value; usually 15, 16, or 32.<br>`Rmask`：the red mask for the format.<br>`Gmask`：the green mask for the format.<br>`Bmask`：the blue mask for the format.<br>`Amask`：the alpha mask for the format.<br>返回：the SDL_PixelFormat value corresponding to the format masks, or SDL_PIXELFORMAT_UNKNOWN if there isn't a match. |
| <a id="api-61f708222a23ad0b"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLPixelFormat Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.GetPixelFormatFromGpuTextureFormat(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGPUTextureFormat format)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L13057) | Get the SDL pixel format corresponding to a GPU texture format. @since This function is available since SDL 3.4.0.<br>`format`：a texture format.<br>返回：the corresponding pixel format, or SDL_PIXELFORMAT_UNKNOWN if there is no corresponding pixel format. |
| <a id="api-2e04e2b3919e5c53"></a>[`public uint Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.GetPrimaryDisplay()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L14523) | Return the primary display. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetDisplays<br>返回：the instance ID of the primary display on success or 0 on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-e7650a198675054c"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGamepadType Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.GetRealGamepadTypeForId(uint instance_id)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L13901) | Get the type of a gamepad, ignoring any mapping override. This can be called before any gamepads are opened. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetGamepadTypeForID @sa SDL_GetGamepads @sa SDL_GetRealGamepadType<br>`instance_id`：the joystick instance ID.<br>返回：the gamepad type. |
| <a id="api-8a44661438e955b8"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSandbox Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.GetSandbox()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L12653) | Get the application sandbox environment, if any. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>返回：the application sandbox environment or SDL_SANDBOX_NONE if the application is not running in a sandbox environment. |
| <a id="api-b65de27e8b40ca7b"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.GetSensorNonPortableTypeForId(uint instance_id)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L12721) | Get the platform dependent type of a sensor. This can be called before any sensors are opened. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`instance_id`：the sensor instance ID.<br>返回：the sensor platform dependent type, or -1 if `instance_id` is not valid. |
| <a id="api-270bdcc785e992cd"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSensorType Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.GetSensorTypeForId(uint instance_id)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L12499) | Get the type of a sensor. This can be called before any sensors are opened. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`instance_id`：the sensor instance ID.<br>返回：the SDL_SensorType, or `SDL_SENSOR_INVALID` if `instance_id` is not valid. |
| <a id="api-64090198a3461c9a"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.GetSilenceValueForFormat(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAudioFormat format)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L14885) | Get the appropriate memset value for silencing an audio format. The value returned by this function can be used as the second argument to memset (or SDL_memset) to set an audio buffer in a specific format to silence. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`format`：the audio data format to query.<br>返回：a byte value that can be passed to memset. |
| <a id="api-0e7ec8e122f9bb15"></a>[`public ulong Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.GetSimdAlignment()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L16183) | Report the alignment this system needs for SIMD allocations. This will return the minimum number of bytes to which a pointer must be aligned to be compatible with SIMD instructions on the current machine. For example, if the machine supports SSE only, it will return 16, but if it supports AVX-512F, it'll return 64 (etc). This only reports values for instruction sets SDL knows about, so if your SDL build doesn't have SDL_HasAVX512F(), then it might return 16 for the SSE support it sees and not 64 for the AVX-512 instructions that exist but SDL doesn't know about. Plan accordingly. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_aligned_alloc @sa SDL_aligned_free<br>返回：the alignment in bytes needed for available, known SIMD instructions. |
| <a id="api-68e737064fe986da"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.GetSystemPageSize()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L13808) | Report the size of a page of memory. Different platforms might have different memory page sizes. In current times, 4 kilobytes is not unusual, but newer systems are moving to larger page sizes, and esoteric platforms might have any unexpected size. Note that this function can return 0, which means SDL can't determine the page size on this platform. It will _not_ set an error string to be retrieved with SDL_GetError() in this case! In this case, defaulting to 4096 is often a reasonable option. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.4.0.<br>返回：the size of a single page of memory, in bytes, or 0 if SDL can't determine this information. |
| <a id="api-c9ac96c7db9e6e1f"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.GetSystemRam()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L16306) | Get the amount of RAM configured in the system. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>返回：the amount of RAM configured in the system in MiB. |
| <a id="api-2a0350a06eda69a2"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSystemTheme Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.GetSystemTheme()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L15054) | Get the current system theme. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>返回：the current system theme, light, dark, or unknown. |
| <a id="api-e85b5e221f2af3cb"></a>[`public ulong Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.GetTicks()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L11916) | Get the number of milliseconds that have elapsed since the SDL library initialization. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetTicksNS<br>返回：an unsigned 64‑bit integer that represents the number of milliseconds that have elapsed since the SDL library was initialized (typically via a call to SDL_Init). |
| <a id="api-b6e78822e074ebdf"></a>[`public ulong Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.GetTicksNs()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L14829) | Get the number of nanoseconds since SDL library initialization. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>返回：an unsigned 64-bit value representing the number of nanoseconds since the SDL library initialized. |
| <a id="api-f40d8ea9616dcac1"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLTouchDeviceType Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.GetTouchDeviceType(ulong touchID)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L13284) | Get the type of the given touch device. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`touchID`：the ID of a touch device.<br>返回：touch device type. |
| <a id="api-0b083be9282b1f40"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.GetVersion()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L14396) | Get the version of SDL that is linked against your program. If you are linking to SDL dynamically, then it is possible that the current version will be different than the version you compiled against. This function returns the current version, while SDL_VERSION is the version you compiled with. This function may be called safely at any time, even before SDL_Init(). @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetRevision<br>返回：the version of the linked library. |
| <a id="api-a7b5abad706214b1"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.GlResetAttributes()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L15273) | Reset all previously set OpenGL context attributes to their default values. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GL_GetAttribute @sa SDL_GL_SetAttribute |
| <a id="api-459b54379ea20e1c"></a>[`public bool Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.GlSetAttribute(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGLAttr attr, int value)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L13661) | Set an OpenGL window attribute before window creation. This function sets the OpenGL attribute `attr` to `value`. The requested attributes should be set before creating an OpenGL window. You should use SDL_GL_GetAttribute() to check the values after creating the OpenGL context, since the values obtained can differ from the requested ones. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GL_CreateContext @sa SDL_GL_GetAttribute @sa SDL_GL_ResetAttributes<br>`attr`：an enum value specifying the OpenGL attribute to set.<br>`value`：the desired value for the attribute.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-2afccc26c6a1448f"></a>[`public bool Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.GlSetSwapInterval(int interval)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L11694) | Set the swap interval for the current OpenGL context. Some systems allow specifying -1 for the interval, to enable adaptive vsync. Adaptive vsync works the same as vsync, but if you've already missed the vertical retrace for a given frame, it swaps buffers immediately, which might be less jarring for the user during occasional framerate drops. If an application requests adaptive vsync and the system does not support it, this function will fail and return false. In such a case, you should probably retry the call with 1 for the interval. Adaptive vsync is implemented for some glX drivers with GLX_EXT_swap_control_tear, and for some Windows drivers with WGL_EXT_swap_control_tear. Read more on the Khronos wiki: https://www.khronos.org/opengl/wiki/Swap_Interval#Adaptive_Vsync @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GL_GetSwapInterval<br>`interval`：0 for immediate updates, 1 for updates synchronized with the vertical retrace, -1 for adaptive vsync.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-97aa90f992477b53"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.GlUnloadLibrary()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L15618) | Unload the OpenGL library previously loaded by SDL_GL_LoadLibrary(). @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GL_LoadLibrary |

| <a id="api-325fe59ac67ae911"></a>[`public uint Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.GpuTextureFormatTexelBlockSize(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGPUTextureFormat format)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L12974) | Obtains the texel block size for a texture format. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_UploadToGPUTexture<br>`format`：the texture format you want to know the texel size of.<br>返回：the texel block size of the texture format. |
| <a id="api-8a2a53117ef1a63b"></a>[`public bool Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.HasAltiVec()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L16602) | Determine whether the CPU has AltiVec features. | <a id="api-543f2a7d178e83af"></a>[`public bool Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.HasArmsimd()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L16159) | Determine whether the CPU has ARM SIMD (ARMv6) features. This is different from ARM NEON, which is a different instruction set. This always returns false on CPUs that aren't using ARM instruction sets. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_HasNEON<br>返回：true if the CPU has ARM SIMD features or false if not. |
| <a id="api-e5201b5e061ae04b"></a>[`public bool Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.HasAvx()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L13339) | Determine whether the CPU has AVX features. This always returns false on CPUs that aren't using Intel instruction sets. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_HasAVX2 @sa SDL_HasAVX512F<br>返回：true if the CPU has AVX features or false if not. |
| <a id="api-8572a2bbb0a56f3d"></a>[`public bool Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.HasAvx2()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L15882) | Determine whether the CPU has AVX2 features. This always returns false on CPUs that aren't using Intel instruction sets. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_HasAVX @sa SDL_HasAVX512F<br>返回：true if the CPU has AVX2 features or false if not. |
| <a id="api-5ca2d8e590e33db5"></a>[`public bool Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.HasAvx512F()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L11430) | Determine whether the CPU has AVX-512F (foundation) features. This always returns false on CPUs that aren't using Intel instruction sets. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_HasAVX @sa SDL_HasAVX2<br>返回：true if the CPU has AVX-512F features or false if not. |
| <a id="api-06b41e46f51e6fa7"></a>[`public bool Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.HasClipboardText()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L15685) | Query whether the clipboard exists and contains a non-empty text string. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetClipboardText @sa SDL_SetClipboardText<br>返回：true if the clipboard has text, or false if it does not. |

If you need to check for a range of event types, use SDL_HasEvents() instead. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_HasEvents<br>`type`：the type of event to be queried; see SDL_EventType for details.<br>返回：true if events matching `type` are present, or false if events matching `type` are not present. |
| <a id="api-70fcf00be8582f48"></a>[`public bool Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.HasEvents(uint minType, uint maxType)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L12284) | Check for the existence of certain event types in the event queue. If you need to check for a single event type, use SDL_HasEvent() instead. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_HasEvents<br>`minType`：the low end of event type to be queried, inclusive; see SDL_EventType for details.<br>`maxType`：the high end of event type to be queried, inclusive; see SDL_EventType for details.<br>返回：true if events with type >= `minType` and < = `maxType` are present, or false if not. |
| <a id="api-b94bacfb2f5cfc21"></a>[`public bool Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.HasGamepad()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L13390) | Return whether a gamepad is currently connected. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetGamepads<br>返回：true if a gamepad is connected, false otherwise. |

This always returns false on CPUs that aren't using Intel instruction sets. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_HasSSE @sa SDL_HasSSE2 @sa SDL_HasSSE41 @sa SDL_HasSSE42<br>返回：true if the CPU has SSE3 features or false if not. |
| <a id="api-bdebc59fda6b11ba"></a>[`public bool Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.HasSse41()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L15703) | Determine whether the CPU has SSE4.1 features. This always returns false on CPUs that aren't using Intel instruction sets. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_HasSSE @sa SDL_HasSSE2 @sa SDL_HasSSE3 @sa SDL_HasSSE42<br>返回：true if the CPU has SSE4.1 features or false if not. |
| <a id="api-9cbc72dccd282871"></a>[`public bool Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.HasSse42()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L14735) | Determine whether the CPU has SSE4.2 features. This always returns false on CPUs that aren't using Intel instruction sets. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_HasSSE @sa SDL_HasSSE2 @sa SDL_HasSSE3 @sa SDL_HasSSE41<br>返回：true if the CPU has SSE4.2 features or false if not. |
| <a id="api-fbf0f20e89330a2e"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.HidBleScan(bool active)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L12958) | Start or stop a BLE scan on iOS and tvOS to pair Steam Controllers. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`active`：true to start the scan, false to stop the scan. |
| <a id="api-73070a1e4e6d64d8"></a>[`public uint Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.HidDeviceChangeCount()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L16071) | Check to see if devices may have been added or removed. Enumerating the HID devices is an expensive operation, so you can call this to see if there have been any system device changes since the last call to this function. A change in the counter returned doesn't necessarily mean that anything has changed, but you can call SDL_hid_enumerate() to get an updated device list. Calling this function for the first time may cause a thread or other system resource to be allocated to track device change notifications. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_hid_enumerate<br>返回：a change counter that is incremented with each potential device change, or 0 if device change detection isn't available. |
| <a id="api-f6b85d7d4cd12508"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.HidExit()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L16199) | Finalize the HIDAPI library. This function frees all of the static data associated with HIDAPI. It should be called at the end of execution to avoid memory leaks. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_hid_init<br>返回：0 on success or a negative error code on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-6ad3539b59a4a5be"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.HidInit()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L16479) | Initialize the HIDAPI library. This function initializes the HIDAPI library. Calling it is not strictly necessary, as it will be called automatically by SDL_hid_enumerate(), SDL_hid_open(), and SDL_hid_open_path() if needed. This function should be called at the beginning of execution however, if there is a chance of HIDAPI handles being opened by different threads simultaneously. Each call to this function should have a matching call to SDL_hid_exit() @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_hid_exit<br>返回：0 on success or a negative error code on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-686aed2f2d1035bb"></a>[`public bool Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.HideCursor()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L13842) | Hide the cursor. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_CursorVisible @sa SDL_ShowCursor<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-962dd2801eedbd17"></a>[`public bool Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.Init(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLInitFlags flags)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L15379) | Initialize the SDL library. SDL_Init() simply forwards to calling SDL_InitSubSystem(). Therefore, the two may be used interchangeably. Though for readability of your code SDL_InitSubSystem() might be preferred. The file I/O (for example: SDL_IOFromFile) and threading (SDL_CreateThread) subsystems are initialized by default. Message boxes (SDL_ShowSimpleMessageBox) also attempt to work without initializing the video subsystem, in hopes of being useful in showing an error dialog when SDL_Init fails. You must specifically initialize other subsystems if you use them in your application. Logging (such as SDL_Log) works without initialization, too. `flags` may be any of the following OR'd together: - `SDL_INIT_AUDIO`: audio subsystem; automatically initializes the events subsystem - `SDL_INIT_VIDEO`: video subsystem; automatically initializes the events subsystem, should be initialized on the main thread. - `SDL_INIT_JOYSTICK`: joystick subsystem; automatically initializes the events subsystem - `SDL_INIT_HAPTIC`: haptic (force feedback) subsystem - `SDL_INIT_GAMEPAD`: gamepad subsystem; automatically initializes the joystick subsystem - `SDL_INIT_EVENTS`: events subsystem - `SDL_INIT_SENSOR`: sensor subsystem; automatically initializes the events subsystem - `SDL_INIT_CAMERA`: camera subsystem; automatically initializes the events subsystem Subsystem initialization is ref-counted, you must call SDL_QuitSubSystem() for each SDL_InitSubSystem() to correctly shutdown a subsystem manually (or call SDL_Quit() to force shutdown). If a subsystem is already loaded then this call will increase the ref-count and return. Consider reporting some basic metadata about your application before calling SDL_Init, using either SDL_SetAppMetadata() or SDL_SetAppMetadataProperty(). @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetAppMetadata @sa SDL_SetAppMetadataProperty @sa SDL_InitSubSystem @sa SDL_Quit @sa SDL_SetMainReady @sa SDL_WasInit<br>`flags`：subsystem initialization flags.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-4f2525d4a6d5b261"></a>[`public bool Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.InitSubSystem(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLInitFlags flags)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L15438) | Compatibility function to initialize the SDL library. This function and SDL_Init() are interchangeable. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_Init @sa SDL_Quit @sa SDL_QuitSubSystem<br>`flags`：any of the flags used by SDL_Init(); see SDL_Init for details.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-39a78f7bfb87b6ca"></a>[`public bool Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.IsAudioDevicePhysical(uint devid)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L14280) | Determine if an audio device is physical (instead of logical). An SDL_AudioDeviceID that represents physical hardware is a physical device; there is one for each piece of hardware that SDL can see. Logical devices are created by calling SDL_OpenAudioDevice or SDL_OpenAudioDeviceStream, and while each is associated with a physical device, there can be any number of logical devices on one physical device. For the most part, logical and physical IDs are interchangeable--if you try to open a logical device, SDL understands to assign that effort to the underlying physical device, etc. However, it might be useful to know if an arbitrary device ID is physical or logical. This function reports which. This function may return either true or false for invalid device IDs. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`devid`：the device ID to query.<br>返回：true if devid is a physical device, false if it is logical. |
| <a id="api-9aa29c34601aab33"></a>[`public bool Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.IsAudioDevicePlayback(uint devid)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L12350) | Determine if an audio device is a playback device (instead of recording). This function may return either true or false for invalid device IDs. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`devid`：the device ID to query.<br>返回：true if devid is a playback device, false if it is recording. |
| <a id="api-aa66125ec2acd046"></a>[`public bool Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.IsGamepad(uint instance_id)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L14631) | Check if the given joystick is supported by the gamepad interface. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetJoysticks @sa SDL_OpenGamepad<br>`instance_id`：the joystick instance ID.<br>返回：true if the given joystick is supported by the gamepad interface, false if it isn't or it's an invalid index. |
| <a id="api-482f1deb2db705ff"></a>[`public bool Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.IsJoystickVirtual(uint instance_id)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L16118) | Query whether or not a joystick is virtual. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`instance_id`：the joystick instance ID.<br>返回：true if the joystick is virtual, false otherwise. |
| <a id="api-b920338027c616c5"></a>[`public bool Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.IsMainThread()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L11859) | Return whether this is the main thread. On Apple platforms, the main thread is the thread that runs your program's main() entry point. On other platforms, the main thread is the one that calls SDL_Init(SDL_INIT_VIDEO), which should usually be the one that runs your program's main() entry point. If you are using the main callbacks, SDL_AppInit(), SDL_AppIterate(), and SDL_AppQuit() are all called on the main thread. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_RunOnMainThread<br>返回：true if this thread is the main thread, or false otherwise. |
| <a id="api-af099ffe509ee124"></a>[`public bool Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.IsMouseHaptic()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L12381) | Query whether or not the current mouse has haptic capabilities. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_OpenHapticFromMouse<br>返回：true if the mouse is haptic or false if it isn't. |
| <a id="api-84692006e1517e81"></a>[`public bool Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.IsTablet()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L12311) | Query if the current device is a tablet. If SDL can't determine this, it will return false. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>返回：true if the device is a tablet, false otherwise. |
| <a id="api-b6ad1f0929d62ad0"></a>[`public bool Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.IsTv()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L11567) | Query if the current device is a TV. If SDL can't determine this, it will return false. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>返回：true if the device is a TV, false otherwise. |
| <a id="api-e1b42b1411a4a7a9"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.Isalnum(int x)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L15397) | Query if a character is alphabetic (a letter) or a number. **WARNING**: Regardless of system locale, this will only treat ASCII values for English 'a-z', 'A-Z', and '0-9' as true. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`x`：character value to check.<br>返回：non-zero if x falls within the character class, zero otherwise. |
| <a id="api-4673237f81fddf46"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.Isalpha(int x)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L16355) | Query if a character is alphabetic (a letter). **WARNING**: Regardless of system locale, this will only treat ASCII values for English 'a-z' and 'A-Z' as true. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`x`：character value to check.<br>返回：non-zero if x falls within the character class, zero otherwise. |

| <a id="api-a37c17b37767d26b"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.Iscntrl(int x)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L16459) | Report if a character is a control character. **WARNING**: Regardless of system locale, this will only treat ASCII values 0 through 0x1F, and 0x7F, as true. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`x`：character value to check.<br>返回：non-zero if x falls within the character class, zero otherwise. |
| <a id="api-d04586f5110e434d"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.Isdigit(int x)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L12368) | Report if a character is a numeric digit. **WARNING**: Regardless of system locale, this will only treat ASCII values '0' (0x30) through '9' (0x39), as true. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`x`：character value to check.<br>返回：non-zero if x falls within the character class, zero otherwise. |
| <a id="api-8e2e6ea64cc016fd"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.Isgraph(int x)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L14177) | Report if a character is any "printable" except space. Be advised that "printable" has a definition that goes back to text terminals from the dawn of computing, making this a sort of special case function that is not suitable for Unicode (or most any) text management. **WARNING**: Regardless of system locale, this is equivalent to `(SDL_isprint(x)) & & ((x) != ' ')`. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_isprint<br>`x`：character value to check.<br>返回：non-zero if x falls within the character class, zero otherwise. |

| <a id="api-df53c739bdea949d"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.Islower(int x)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L14541) | Report if a character is lower case. **WARNING**: Regardless of system locale, this will only treat ASCII values 'a' through 'z' as true. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`x`：character value to check.<br>返回：non-zero if x falls within the character class, zero otherwise. |
| <a id="api-d587911594d8d3d7"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.Isnan(double x)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L15653) | Return whether the value is NaN. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_isnanf<br>`x`：double-precision floating point value.<br>返回：non-zero if the value is NaN, 0 otherwise. |

Be advised that "printable" has a definition that goes back to text terminals from the dawn of computing, making this a sort of special case function that is not suitable for Unicode (or most any) text management. **WARNING**: Regardless of system locale, this will only treat ASCII values ' ' (0x20) through '~' (0x7E) as true. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`x`：character value to check.<br>返回：non-zero if x falls within the character class, zero otherwise. |
| <a id="api-c3e1977f58353639"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.Ispunct(int x)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L15041) | Report if a character is a punctuation mark. **WARNING**: Regardless of system locale, this is equivalent to `((SDL_isgraph(x)) & & (!SDL_isalnum(x)))`. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_isgraph @sa SDL_isalnum<br>`x`：character value to check.<br>返回：non-zero if x falls within the character class, zero otherwise. |
| <a id="api-5954028953b5ae8a"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.Isspace(int x)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L15261) | Report if a character is whitespace. **WARNING**: Regardless of system locale, this will only treat the following ASCII values as true: - space (0x20) - tab (0x09) - newline (0x0A) - vertical tab (0x0B) - form feed (0x0C) - return (0x0D) @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`x`：character value to check.<br>返回：non-zero if x falls within the character class, zero otherwise. |

| <a id="api-38058a0ea7ce54ef"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.LockJoysticks()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L11991) | Locking for atomic access to the joystick API. The SDL joystick functions are thread-safe, however you can lock the joysticks while processing to guarantee that the joystick list won't change and joystick and gamepad events will not be delivered. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. |
| <a id="api-23540283f2c2eabf"></a>[`public bool Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.LockProperties(uint props)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L15960) | Lock a group of properties. Obtain a multi-threaded lock for these properties. Other threads will wait while trying to lock these properties until they are unlocked. Properties must be unlocked before they are destroyed. The lock is automatically taken when setting individual properties, this function is only needed when you want to set several properties atomically or want to guarantee that properties being queried aren't freed in another thread. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_UnlockProperties<br>`props`：the properties to lock.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-6b738f3e852ae6d0"></a>[`public double Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.Log(double x)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L11553) | Compute the natural logarithm of `x`. Domain: `0 < x < = INF` Range: `-INF < = y < = INF` It is an error for `x` to be less than or equal to 0. This function operates on double-precision floating point values, use SDL_logf for single-precision floats. This function may use a different approximation across different versions, platforms and configurations. i.e, it can return a different value given the same input on different machines or operating systems, or if SDL is updated. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_logf @sa SDL_log10 @sa SDL_exp<br>`x`：floating point value. Must be greater than 0.<br>返回：the natural logarithm of `x`. |
| <a id="api-b9109ad5aa2fef17"></a>[`public double Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.Log10(double x)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L15739) | Compute the base-10 logarithm of `x`. Domain: `0 < x < = INF` Range: `-INF < = y < = INF` It is an error for `x` to be less than or equal to 0. This function operates on double-precision floating point values, use SDL_log10f for single-precision floats. This function may use a different approximation across different versions, platforms and configurations. i.e, it can return a different value given the same input on different machines or operating systems, or if SDL is updated. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_log10f @sa SDL_log @sa SDL_pow<br>`x`：floating point value. Must be greater than 0.<br>返回：the logarithm of `x`. |
| <a id="api-f7f76f0685e8897a"></a>[`public float Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.Log10f(float x)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L14048) | Compute the base-10 logarithm of `x`. Domain: `0 < x < = INF` Range: `-INF < = y < = INF` It is an error for `x` to be less than or equal to 0. This function operates on single-precision floating point values, use SDL_log10 for double-precision floats. This function may use a different approximation across different versions, platforms and configurations. i.e, it can return a different value given the same input on different machines or operating systems, or if SDL is updated. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_log10 @sa SDL_logf @sa SDL_powf<br>`x`：floating point value. Must be greater than 0.<br>返回：the logarithm of `x`. |
| <a id="api-b68877d537b8940e"></a>[`public float Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.Logf(float x)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L11729) | Compute the natural logarithm of `x`. | <a id="api-a2ee0e4b32213887"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.MemoryBarrierAcquireFunction()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L14926) | Insert a memory acquire barrier (function version). Please refer to SDL_MemoryBarrierRelease for details. This is a function version, which might be useful if you need to use this functionality from a scripting language, etc. Also, some of the macro versions call this function behind the scenes, where more heavy lifting can happen inside of SDL. Generally, though, an app written in C/C++/etc should use the macro version, as it will be more efficient. @threadsafety Obviously this function is safe to use from any thread at any time, but if you find yourself needing this, you are probably dealing with some very sensitive code; be careful! @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_MemoryBarrierAcquire |
| <a id="api-df247ebaf4168e25"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.MemoryBarrierReleaseFunction()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L13732) | Insert a memory release barrier (function version). Please refer to SDL_MemoryBarrierRelease for details. This is a function version, which might be useful if you need to use this functionality from a scripting language, etc. Also, some of the macro versions call this function behind the scenes, where more heavy lifting can happen inside of SDL. Generally, though, an app written in C/C++/etc should use the macro version, as it will be more efficient. @threadsafety Obviously this function is safe to use from any thread at any time, but if you find yourself needing this, you are probably dealing with some very sensitive code; be careful! @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_MemoryBarrierRelease |
| <a id="api-43defddb7c6d964a"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.OnApplicationDidEnterBackground()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L14254) | Let iOS apps with external event handling report onApplicationDidEnterBackground. This functions allows iOS apps that have their own event handling to hook into SDL to generate SDL events. This maps directly to an iOS-specific event, but since it doesn't do anything iOS-specific internally, it is available on all platforms, in case it might be useful for some specific paradigm. Most apps do not need to use this directly; SDL's internal event code will handle all this for windows created by SDL_CreateWindow! @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. |
