| <a id="api-92fb1c8089d23758"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.EnumerateDirectory(sbyte* path, delegate* unmanaged[Cdecl]<void*, sbyte*, sbyte*, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLEnumerationResult> callback, void* userdata)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L27515) | Enumerate a directory through a callback function. This function provides every directory entry through an app-provided callback, called once for each directory entry, until all results have been provided or the callback returns either SDL_ENUM_SUCCESS or SDL_ENUM_FAILURE. This will return false if there was a system problem in general, or if a callback returns SDL_ENUM_FAILURE. A successful return means a callback returned SDL_ENUM_SUCCESS to halt enumeration, or all directory entries were enumerated. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`path`：the path of the directory to enumerate.<br>`callback`：a function that is called for each entry in the directory.<br>`userdata`：a pointer that is passed to `callback`.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-18d1af326db098fb"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.EnumerateProperties(uint props, delegate* unmanaged[Cdecl]<void*, uint, sbyte*, void> callback, void* userdata)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L38490) | Enumerate the properties contained in a group of properties. The callback function is called for each property in the group of properties. The properties are locked during enumeration. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`props`：the properties to query.<br>`callback`：the function to call for each property.<br>`userdata`：a pointer that is passed to `callback`.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-634a30a5f0d2348b"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.EnumerateStorageDirectory(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLStorage* storage, sbyte* path, delegate* unmanaged[Cdecl]<void*, sbyte*, sbyte*, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLEnumerationResult> callback, void* userdata)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L49417) | Enumerate a directory in a storage container through a callback function. This function provides every directory entry through an app-provided callback, called once for each directory entry, until all results have been provided or the callback returns either SDL_ENUM_SUCCESS or SDL_ENUM_FAILURE. This will return false if there was a system problem in general, or if a callback returns SDL_ENUM_FAILURE. A successful return means a callback returned SDL_ENUM_SUCCESS to halt enumeration, or all directory entries were enumerated. If `path` is NULL, this is treated as a request to enumerate the root of the storage container's tree. An empty string also works for this. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_StorageReady<br>`storage`：a storage container.<br>`path`：the path of the directory to enumerate, or NULL for the root.<br>`callback`：a function that is called for each entry in the directory.<br>`userdata`：a pointer that is passed to `callback`.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-4487bd0361632db4"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.EventEnabled(uint type)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L31251) | Query the state of processing events by type. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetEventEnabled<br>`type`：the type of event; see SDL_EventType for details.<br>返回：true if the event is being processed, false otherwise. |
| <a id="api-71d9da4240125bf1"></a>[`public double Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Exp(double x)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L16701) | Compute the exponential of `x`. The definition of `y = exp(x)` is `y = e^x`, where `e` is the base of the natural logarithm. The inverse is the natural logarithm, SDL_log. Domain: `-INF < = x < = INF` Range: `0 < = y < = INF` The output will overflow if `exp(x)` is too large to be represented. This function operates on double-precision floating point values, use SDL_expf for single-precision floats. This function may use a different approximation across different versions, platforms and configurations. i.e, it can return a different value given the same input on different machines or operating systems, or if SDL is updated. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_expf @sa SDL_log<br>`x`：floating point value.<br>返回：value of `e^x`. |
| <a id="api-c8c14570633c223d"></a>[`public float Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Expf(float x)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L38830) | Compute the exponential of `x`. The definition of `y = exp(x)` is `y = e^x`, where `e` is the base of the natural logarithm. The inverse is the natural logarithm, SDL_logf. Domain: `-INF < = x < = INF` Range: `0 < = y < = INF` The output will overflow if `exp(x)` is too large to be represented. This function operates on single-precision floating point values, use SDL_exp for double-precision floats. This function may use a different approximation across different versions, platforms and configurations. i.e, it can return a different value given the same input on different machines or operating systems, or if SDL is updated. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_exp @sa SDL_logf<br>`x`：floating point value.<br>返回：value of `e^x`. |
| <a id="api-83b9e81225c72267"></a>[`public double Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Fabs(double x)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L20208) | Compute the absolute value of `x` Domain: `-INF < = x < = INF` Range: `0 < = y < = INF` This function operates on double-precision floating point values, use SDL_fabsf for single-precision floats. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_fabsf<br>`x`：floating point value to use as the magnitude.<br>返回：the absolute value of `x`. |
| <a id="api-2db7fd4ae171411a"></a>[`public float Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Fabsf(float x)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L18557) | Compute the absolute value of `x` Domain: `-INF < = x < = INF` Range: `0 < = y < = INF` This function operates on single-precision floating point values, use SDL_fabs for double-precision floats. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_fabs<br>`x`：floating point value to use as the magnitude.<br>返回：the absolute value of `x`. |
| <a id="api-9deb18bf8e80ef47"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.FillSurfaceRect(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSurface* dst, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRect* rect, uint color)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L25256) | Perform a fast fill of a rectangle with a specific color. `color` should be a pixel of the format used by the surface, and can be generated by SDL_MapRGB() or SDL_MapRGBA(). If the color value contains an alpha component then the destination is simply filled with that alpha information, no blending takes place. If there is a clip rectangle set on the destination (set via SDL_SetSurfaceClipRect()), then this function will fill based on the intersection of the clip rectangle and `rect`. @threadsafety This function can be called on different threads with different surfaces. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_FillSurfaceRects<br>`dst`：the SDL_Surface structure that is the drawing target.<br>`rect`：the SDL_Rect structure representing the rectangle to fill, or NULL to fill the entire surface.<br>`color`：the color to fill with.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-0cf13f54f6678f27"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.FillSurfaceRects(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSurface* dst, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRect* rects, int count, uint color)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L38312) | Perform a fast fill of a set of rectangles with a specific color. `color` should be a pixel of the format used by the surface, and can be generated by SDL_MapRGB() or SDL_MapRGBA(). If the color value contains an alpha component then the destination is simply filled with that alpha information, no blending takes place. If there is a clip rectangle set on the destination (set via SDL_SetSurfaceClipRect()), then this function will fill based on the intersection of the clip rectangle and `rect`. @threadsafety This function can be called on different threads with different surfaces. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_FillSurfaceRect<br>`dst`：the SDL_Surface structure that is the drawing target.<br>`rects`：an array of SDL_Rects representing the rectangles to fill.<br>`count`：the number of rectangles in the array.<br>`color`：the color to fill with.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-cfda7bf0f4a98c8d"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.FilterEvents(delegate* unmanaged[Cdecl]<void*, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLEvent*, byte> filter, void* userdata)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L45023) | Run a specific filter function on the current event queue, removing any events for which the filter returns false. See SDL_SetEventFilter() for more information. Unlike SDL_SetEventFilter(), this function does not change the filter permanently, it only uses the supplied filter until this function returns. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetEventFilter @sa SDL_SetEventFilter<br>`filter`：the SDL_EventFilter function to call when an event happens.<br>`userdata`：a pointer that is passed to `filter`. |
| <a id="api-590147e7cd5c9c37"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.FlashWindow(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLFlashOperation operation)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L22524) | Request a window to demand attention from the user. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`window`：the window to be flashed.<br>`operation`：the operation to perform.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-7b452aaa938098df"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.FlipSurface(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSurface* surface, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLFlipMode flip)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L45353) | Flip a surface vertically or horizontally. @threadsafety This function can be called on different threads with different surfaces. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`surface`：the surface to flip.<br>`flip`：the direction to flip.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-8a6ad455975ecb5a"></a>[`public double Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Floor(double x)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L39487) | Compute the floor of `x`. The floor of `x` is the largest integer `y` such that `y < = x`, i.e `x` rounded down to the nearest integer. Domain: `-INF < = x < = INF` Range: `-INF < = y < = INF`, y integer This function operates on double-precision floating point values, use SDL_floorf for single-precision floats. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_floorf @sa SDL_ceil @sa SDL_trunc @sa SDL_round @sa SDL_lround<br>`x`：floating point value.<br>返回：the floor of `x`. |
| <a id="api-ef48cf9492cdf3ef"></a>[`public float Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Floorf(float x)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L47163) | Compute the floor of `x`. The floor of `x` is the largest integer `y` such that `y < = x`, i.e `x` rounded down to the nearest integer. Domain: `-INF < = x < = INF` Range: `-INF < = y < = INF`, y integer This function operates on single-precision floating point values, use SDL_floor for double-precision floats. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_floor @sa SDL_ceilf @sa SDL_truncf @sa SDL_roundf @sa SDL_lroundf<br>`x`：floating point value.<br>返回：the floor of `x`. |
| <a id="api-d5f22506e82de242"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.FlushAudioStream(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAudioStream* stream)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L28705) | Tell the stream that you're done sending data, and anything being buffered should be converted/resampled and made available immediately. It is legal to add more data to a stream after flushing, but there may be audio gaps in the output. Generally this is intended to signal the end of input, so the complete output becomes available. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_PutAudioStreamData<br>`stream`：the audio stream to flush.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-acf0e347e86a0fde"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.FlushEvent(uint type)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L52640) | Clear events of a specific type from the event queue. This will unconditionally remove any events from the queue that match `type`. If you need to remove a range of event types, use SDL_FlushEvents() instead. It's also normal to just ignore events you don't care about in your event loop without calling this function. This function only affects currently queued events. If you want to make sure that all pending OS events are flushed, you can call SDL_PumpEvents() on the main thread immediately before the flush call. If you have user events with custom data that needs to be freed, you should use SDL_PeepEvents() to remove and clean up those events before calling this function. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_FlushEvents<br>`type`：the type of event to be cleared; see SDL_EventType for details. |
| <a id="api-f3c4f0aed998adf2"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.FlushEvents(uint minType, uint maxType)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L49020) | Clear events of a range of types from the event queue. This will unconditionally remove any events from the queue that are in the range of `minType` to `maxType`, inclusive. If you need to remove a single event type, use SDL_FlushEvent() instead. It's also normal to just ignore events you don't care about in your event loop without calling this function. This function only affects currently queued events. If you want to make sure that all pending OS events are flushed, you can call SDL_PumpEvents() on the main thread immediately before the flush call. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_FlushEvent<br>`minType`：the low end of event type to be cleared, inclusive; see SDL_EventType for details.<br>`maxType`：the high end of event type to be cleared, inclusive; see SDL_EventType for details. |
| <a id="api-f81bb89d3e6c2ef2"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.FlushIo(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLIOStream* context)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L29890) | Flush any buffered data in the stream. This function makes sure that any buffered data is written to the stream. Normally this isn't necessary but if the stream is a pipe or socket it guarantees that any pending data is sent. @threadsafety Do not use the same SDL_IOStream from two threads at once. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_OpenIO @sa SDL_WriteIO<br>`context`：SDL_IOStream structure to flush.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-3155b62a5fc8a6e5"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.FlushRenderer(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRenderer* renderer)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L29459) | Force the rendering context to flush any pending commands and state. You do not need to (and in fact, shouldn't) call this function unless you are planning to call into OpenGL/Direct3D/Metal/whatever directly, in addition to using an SDL_Renderer. This is for a very-specific case: if you are using SDL's render API, and you plan to make OpenGL/D3D/whatever calls in addition to SDL render API calls. If this applies, you should call this function between calls to SDL's render API and the low-level API you're using in cooperation. In all other cases, you can ignore this function. This call makes SDL flush any pending rendering work it was queueing up to do later in a single batch, and marks any internal cached state as invalid, so it'll prepare all its state again later, from scratch. This means you do not need to save state in your rendering code to protect the SDL renderer. However, there lots of arbitrary pieces of Direct3D and OpenGL state that can confuse things; you should use your best judgment and be prepared to make changes if specific state needs to be protected. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`renderer`：the rendering context.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-91d059ce70cc86c7"></a>[`public double Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Fmod(double x, double y)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L35978) | Return the floating-point remainder of `x / y` Divides `x` by `y`, and returns the remainder. Domain: `-INF < = x < = INF`, `-INF < = y < = INF`, `y != 0` Range: `-y < = z < = y` This function operates on double-precision floating point values, use SDL_fmodf for single-precision floats. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_fmodf @sa SDL_modf @sa SDL_trunc @sa SDL_ceil @sa SDL_floor @sa SDL_round @sa SDL_lround<br>`x`：the numerator.<br>`y`：the denominator. Must not be 0.<br>返回：the remainder of `x / y`. |
| <a id="api-7028b294b0cc645f"></a>[`public float Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Fmodf(float x, float y)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L26426) | Return the floating-point remainder of `x / y` Divides `x` by `y`, and returns the remainder. Domain: `-INF < = x < = INF`, `-INF < = y < = INF`, `y != 0` Range: `-y < = z < = y` This function operates on single-precision floating point values, use SDL_fmod for double-precision floats. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_fmod @sa SDL_truncf @sa SDL_modff @sa SDL_ceilf @sa SDL_floorf @sa SDL_roundf @sa SDL_lroundf<br>`x`：the numerator.<br>`y`：the denominator. Must not be 0.<br>返回：the remainder of `x / y`. |
| <a id="api-7112ad99689f4b5d"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Free(void* mem)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L42084) | Free allocated memory. | <a id="api-ddbcf48eb06b5a31"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GamepadHasAxis(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGamepad* gamepad, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGamepadAxis axis)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L52666) | Query whether a gamepad has a given axis. | <a id="api-2668e4188a7fc4d5"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GamepadSensorEnabled(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGamepad* gamepad, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSensorType type)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L35728) | Query whether sensor data reporting is enabled for a gamepad. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetGamepadSensorEnabled<br>`gamepad`：the gamepad to query.<br>`type`：the type of sensor to query.<br>返回：true if the sensor is enabled, false otherwise. |
| <a id="api-1e8492b6ce067da6"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GenerateMipmapsForGpuTexture(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGPUCommandBuffer* command_buffer, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGPUTexture* texture)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L26332) | Generates mipmaps for the given texture. This function must not be called inside of any pass. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`command_buffer`：a command_buffer.<br>`texture`：a texture with more than 1 mip level. |
| <a id="api-1c2f452225b9a657"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetAppMetadataProperty(sbyte* name)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L35199) | Get metadata about your app. This returns metadata previously set using SDL_SetAppMetadata() or SDL_SetAppMetadataProperty(). See SDL_SetAppMetadataProperty() for the list of available properties and their meanings. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread, although the string returned is not protected and could potentially be freed if you call SDL_SetAppMetadataProperty() to set that property from another thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetAppMetadata @sa SDL_SetAppMetadataProperty<br>`name`：the name of the metadata property to get.<br>返回：the current value of the metadata property, or the default if it is not set, NULL for properties with no default. |
| <a id="api-41e778947050cc30"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetAsyncIoResult(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAsyncIOQueue* queue, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAsyncIOOutcome* outcome)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L18822) | Query an async I/O task queue for completed tasks. If a task assigned to this queue has finished, this will return true and fill in `outcome` with the details of the task. If no task in the queue has finished, this function will return false. This function does not block. If a task has completed, this function will free its resources and the task pointer will no longer be valid. The task will be removed from the queue. It is safe for multiple threads to call this function on the same queue at once; a completed task will only go to one of the threads. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_WaitAsyncIOResult<br>`queue`：the async I/O task queue to query.<br>`outcome`：details of a finished task will be written here. May not be NULL.<br>返回：true if a task has completed, false otherwise. |
| <a id="api-04878190dd08c678"></a>[`public long Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetAsyncIoSize(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAsyncIO* asyncio)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L24851) | Use this function to get the size of the data stream in an SDL_AsyncIO. This call is _not_ asynchronous; it assumes that obtaining this info is a non-blocking operation in most reasonable cases. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`asyncio`：the SDL_AsyncIO to get the size of the data stream from.<br>返回：the size of the data stream in the SDL_IOStream on success or a negative error code on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-ccb3ecef19725759"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetAtomicInt(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAtomicInt* a)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L43565) | Get the value of an atomic variable. ***Note: If you don't know what this function is for, you shouldn't use it!*** @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetAtomicInt<br>`a`：a pointer to an SDL_AtomicInt variable.<br>返回：the current value of an atomic variable. |
| <a id="api-a5cb11a362fd0cbc"></a>[`public void* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetAtomicPointer(void** a)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L37384) | Get the value of a pointer atomically. ***Note: If you don't know what this function is for, you shouldn't use it!*** @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_CompareAndSwapAtomicPointer @sa SDL_SetAtomicPointer<br>`a`：a pointer to a pointer.<br>返回：the current value of a pointer. |
| <a id="api-92af1321dd2dda17"></a>[`public uint Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetAtomicU32(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAtomicU32* a)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L28523) | Get the value of an atomic variable. ***Note: If you don't know what this function is for, you shouldn't use it!*** @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetAtomicU32<br>`a`：a pointer to an SDL_AtomicU32 variable.<br>返回：the current value of an atomic variable. |
| <a id="api-473a5f2a3ce74b0a"></a>[`public int* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetAudioDeviceChannelMap(uint devid, int* count)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L41825) | Get the current channel map of an audio device. Channel maps are optional; most things do not need them, instead passing data in the order that SDL expects. Audio devices usually have no remapping applied. This is represented by returning NULL, and does not signify an error. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetAudioStreamInputChannelMap<br>`devid`：the instance ID of the device to query.<br>`count`：On output, set to number of channels in the map. Can be NULL.<br>返回：an array of the current channel mapping, with as many elements as the current output spec's channels, or NULL if default. This should be freed with SDL_free() when it is no longer needed. |
| <a id="api-dc7dd578a32c9448"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetAudioDeviceFormat(uint devid, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAudioSpec* spec, int* sample_frames)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L35789) | Get the current audio format of a specific audio device. For an opened device, this will report the format the device is currently using. If the device isn't yet opened, this will report the device's preferred format (or a reasonable default if this can't be determined). You may also specify SDL_AUDIO_DEVICE_DEFAULT_PLAYBACK or SDL_AUDIO_DEVICE_DEFAULT_RECORDING here, which is useful for getting a reasonable recommendation before opening the system-recommended default device. You can also use this to request the current device buffer size. This is specified in sample frames and represents the amount of data SDL will feed to the physical hardware in each chunk. This can be converted to milliseconds of audio with the following equation: `ms = (int) ((((Sint64) frames) * 1000) / spec.freq);` Buffer size is only important if you need low-level control over the audio playback timing. Most apps do not need this. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`devid`：the instance ID of the device to query.<br>`spec`：on return, will be filled with device details.<br>`sample_frames`：pointer to store device buffer size, in sample frames. Can be NULL.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-aa01ff675ab53453"></a>[`public float Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetAudioDeviceGain(uint devid)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L20892) | Get the gain of an audio device. The gain of a device is its volume; a larger gain means a louder output, with a gain of zero being silence. Audio devices default to a gain of 1.0f (no change in output). Physical devices may not have their gain changed, only logical devices, and this function will always return -1.0f when used on physical devices. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetAudioDeviceGain<br>`devid`：the audio device to query.<br>返回：the gain of the device or -1.0f on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-f2d38de0570898e7"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetAudioDeviceName(uint devid)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L36192) | Get the human-readable name of a specific audio device. **WARNING**: this function will work with SDL_AUDIO_DEVICE_DEFAULT_PLAYBACK and SDL_AUDIO_DEVICE_DEFAULT_RECORDING, returning the current default physical devices' names. However, as the default device may change at any time, it is likely better to show a generic name to the user, like "System default audio device" or perhaps "default [currently %s]". Do not store this name to disk to reidentify the device in a later run of the program, as the default might change in general, and the string will be the name of a specific device and not the abstract system default. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetAudioPlaybackDevices @sa SDL_GetAudioRecordingDevices<br>`devid`：the instance ID of the device to query.<br>返回：the name of the audio device, or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-26347e3d4bc283cd"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetAudioDriver(int index)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L48767) | Use this function to get the name of a built in audio driver. The list of audio drivers is given in the order that they are normally initialized by default; the drivers that seem more reasonable to choose first (as far as the SDL developers believe) are earlier in the list. The names of drivers are all simple, low-ASCII identifiers, like "alsa", "coreaudio" or "wasapi". These never have Unicode characters, and are not meant to be proper names. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetNumAudioDrivers<br>`index`：the index of the audio driver; the value ranges from 0 to SDL_GetNumAudioDrivers() - 1.<br>返回：the name of the audio driver at the requested index, or NULL if an invalid index was specified. |
| <a id="api-32bb694edb874412"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetAudioFormatName(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAudioFormat format)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L18291) | Get the human readable name of an audio format. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`format`：the audio format to query.<br>返回：the human readable name of the specified audio format or "SDL_AUDIO_UNKNOWN" if the format isn't recognized. |
| <a id="api-67315f1c9361e609"></a>[`public uint* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetAudioPlaybackDevices(int* count)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L22921) | Get a list of currently-connected audio playback devices. This returns of list of available devices that play sound, perhaps to speakers or headphones ("playback" devices). If you want devices that record audio, like a microphone ("recording" devices), use SDL_GetAudioRecordingDevices() instead. This only returns a list of physical devices; it will not have any device IDs returned by SDL_OpenAudioDevice(). If this function returns NULL, to signify an error, `*count` will be set to zero. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_OpenAudioDevice @sa SDL_GetAudioRecordingDevices<br>`count`：a pointer filled in with the number of devices returned, may be NULL.<br>返回：a 0 terminated array of device instance IDs or NULL on error; call SDL_GetError() for more information. This should be freed with SDL_free() when it is no longer needed. |
| <a id="api-2b87323f4f8d7a03"></a>[`public uint* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetAudioRecordingDevices(int* count)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L21682) | Get a list of currently-connected audio recording devices. This returns of list of available devices that record audio, like a microphone ("recording" devices). If you want devices that play sound, perhaps to speakers or headphones ("playback" devices), use SDL_GetAudioPlaybackDevices() instead. This only returns a list of physical devices; it will not have any device IDs returned by SDL_OpenAudioDevice(). If this function returns NULL, to signify an error, `*count` will be set to zero. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_OpenAudioDevice @sa SDL_GetAudioPlaybackDevices<br>`count`：a pointer filled in with the number of devices returned, may be NULL.<br>返回：a 0 terminated array of device instance IDs, or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. This should be freed with SDL_free() when it is no longer needed. |
| <a id="api-c15f86caabc1f058"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetAudioStreamAvailable(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAudioStream* stream)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L20458) | Get the number of converted/resampled bytes available. The stream may be buffering data behind the scenes until it has enough to resample correctly, so this number might be lower than what you expect, or even be zero. Add more data or flush the stream if you need the data now. If the stream has so much data that it would overflow an int, the return value is clamped to a maximum value, but no queued data is lost; if there are gigabytes of data queued, the app might need to read some of it with SDL_GetAudioStreamData before this function's return value is no longer clamped. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetAudioStreamData @sa SDL_PutAudioStreamData<br>`stream`：the audio stream to query.<br>返回：the number of converted/resampled bytes available or -1 on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-7199882240d8def2"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetAudioStreamData(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAudioStream* stream, void* buf, int len)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L47913) | Get converted/resampled data from the stream. The input/output data format/channels/samplerate is specified when creating the stream, and can be changed after creation by calling SDL_SetAudioStreamFormat. Note that any conversion and resampling necessary is done during this call, and SDL_PutAudioStreamData simply queues unconverted data for later. This is different than SDL2, where that work was done while inputting new data to the stream and requesting the output just copied the converted data. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread, but if the stream has a callback set, the caller might need to manage extra locking. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_ClearAudioStream @sa SDL_GetAudioStreamAvailable @sa SDL_PutAudioStreamData<br>`stream`：the stream the audio is being requested from.<br>`buf`：a buffer to fill with audio data.<br>`len`：the maximum number of bytes to fill.<br>返回：the number of bytes read from the stream or -1 on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-8c8e617952bcf9da"></a>[`public uint Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetAudioStreamDevice(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAudioStream* stream)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L29353) | Query an audio stream for its currently-bound device. This reports the logical audio device that an audio stream is currently bound to. If not bound, or invalid, this returns zero, which is not a valid device ID. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_BindAudioStream @sa SDL_BindAudioStreams<br>`stream`：the audio stream to query.<br>返回：the bound audio device, or 0 if not bound or invalid. |
| <a id="api-1aa3c266f60081e1"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetAudioStreamFormat(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAudioStream* stream, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAudioSpec* src_spec, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAudioSpec* dst_spec)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L46040) | Query the current format of an audio stream. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread, as it holds a stream-specific mutex while running. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetAudioStreamFormat<br>`stream`：the SDL_AudioStream to query.<br>`src_spec`：where to store the input audio format; ignored if NULL.<br>`dst_spec`：where to store the output audio format; ignored if NULL.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-a5286e1ce3c378ca"></a>[`public float Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetAudioStreamFrequencyRatio(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAudioStream* stream)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L29565) | Get the frequency ratio of an audio stream. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread, as it holds a stream-specific mutex while running. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetAudioStreamFrequencyRatio<br>`stream`：the SDL_AudioStream to query.<br>返回：the frequency ratio of the stream or 0.0 on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-d09472d03d3f5b2b"></a>[`public float Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetAudioStreamGain(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAudioStream* stream)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L26818) | Get the gain of an audio stream. The gain of a stream is its volume; a larger gain means a louder output, with a gain of zero being silence. Audio streams default to a gain of 1.0f (no change in output). @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread, as it holds a stream-specific mutex while running. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetAudioStreamGain<br>`stream`：the SDL_AudioStream to query.<br>返回：the gain of the stream or -1.0f on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-ab668b49af47d622"></a>[`public int* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetAudioStreamInputChannelMap(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAudioStream* stream, int* count)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L31208) | Get the current input channel map of an audio stream. Channel maps are optional; most things do not need them, instead passing data in the order that SDL expects. Audio streams default to no remapping applied. This is represented by returning NULL, and does not signify an error. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread, as it holds a stream-specific mutex while running. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetAudioStreamInputChannelMap<br>`stream`：the SDL_AudioStream to query.<br>`count`：On output, set to number of channels in the map. Can be NULL.<br>返回：an array of the current channel mapping, with as many elements as the current output spec's channels, or NULL if default. This should be freed with SDL_free() when it is no longer needed. |
| <a id="api-ac87e0ccc2510317"></a>[`public int* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetAudioStreamOutputChannelMap(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAudioStream* stream, int* count)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L25197) | Get the current output channel map of an audio stream. Channel maps are optional; most things do not need them, instead passing data in the order that SDL expects. Audio streams default to no remapping applied. This is represented by returning NULL, and does not signify an error. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread, as it holds a stream-specific mutex while running. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetAudioStreamInputChannelMap<br>`stream`：the SDL_AudioStream to query.<br>`count`：On output, set to number of channels in the map. Can be NULL.<br>返回：an array of the current channel mapping, with as many elements as the current output spec's channels, or NULL if default. This should be freed with SDL_free() when it is no longer needed. |
| <a id="api-eaf03a043d63c562"></a>[`public uint Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetAudioStreamProperties(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAudioStream* stream)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L52113) | Get the properties associated with an audio stream. The application can hang any data it wants here, but the following properties are understood by SDL: - `SDL_PROP_AUDIOSTREAM_AUTO_CLEANUP_BOOLEAN`: if true (the default), the stream be automatically cleaned up when the audio subsystem quits. If set to false, the streams will persist beyond that. This property is ignored for streams created through SDL_OpenAudioDeviceStream(), and will always be cleaned up. Streams that are not cleaned up will still be unbound from devices when the audio subsystem quits. This property was added in SDL 3.4.0. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`stream`：the SDL_AudioStream to query.<br>返回：a valid property ID on success or 0 on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-af396edbab2aa64c"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetAudioStreamQueued(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAudioStream* stream)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L27457) | Get the number of bytes currently queued. This is the number of bytes put into a stream as input, not the number that can be retrieved as output. Because of several details, it's not possible to calculate one number directly from the other. If you need to know how much usable data can be retrieved right now, you should use SDL_GetAudioStreamAvailable() and not this function. Note that audio streams can change their input format at any time, even if there is still data queued in a different format, so the returned byte count will not necessarily match the number of _sample frames_ available. Users of this API should be aware of format changes they make when feeding a stream and plan accordingly. Queued data is not converted until it is consumed by SDL_GetAudioStreamData, so this value should be representative of the exact data that was put into the stream. If the stream has so much data that it would overflow an int, the return value is clamped to a maximum value, but no queued data is lost; if there are gigabytes of data queued, the app might need to read some of it with SDL_GetAudioStreamData before this function's return value is no longer clamped. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_PutAudioStreamData @sa SDL_ClearAudioStream<br>`stream`：the audio stream to query.<br>返回：the number of bytes queued or -1 on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-60fb74e183c9942c"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetBasePath()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L50987) | Get the directory where the application was run from. SDL caches the result of this call internally, but the first call to this function is not necessarily fast, so plan accordingly. **macOS and iOS Specific Functionality**: If the application is in a ".app" bundle, this function returns the Resource directory (e.g. MyApp.app/Contents/Resources/). This behaviour can be overridden by adding a property to the Info.plist file. Adding a string key with the name SDL_FILESYSTEM_BASE_DIR_TYPE with a supported value will change the behaviour. Supported values for the SDL_FILESYSTEM_BASE_DIR_TYPE property (Given an application in /Applications/SDLApp/MyApp.app): - `resource`: bundle resource directory (the default). For example: `/Applications/SDLApp/MyApp.app/Contents/Resources` - `bundle`: the Bundle directory. For example: `/Applications/SDLApp/MyApp.app/` - `parent`: the containing directory of the bundle. For example: `/Applications/SDLApp/` **Android Specific Functionality**: This function returns "./", which allows filesystem operations to use internal storage and the asset system. **Nintendo 3DS Specific Functionality**: This function returns "romfs" directory of the application as it is uncommon to store resources outside the executable. As such it is not a writable directory. The returned path is guaranteed to end with a path separator (' \ ' on Windows, '/' on most other platforms). @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetPrefPath<br>返回：an absolute path in UTF-8 encoding to the application data directory. NULL will be returned on error or when the platform doesn't implement this functionality, call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-b60307ce919d765f"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetBooleanProperty(uint props, sbyte* name, byte default_value)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L17290) | Get a boolean property from a group of properties. You can use SDL_GetPropertyType() to query whether the property exists and is a boolean property. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetPropertyType @sa SDL_HasProperty @sa SDL_SetBooleanProperty<br>`props`：the properties to query.<br>`name`：the name of the property to query.<br>`default_value`：the default value of the property.<br>返回：the value of the property, or `default_value` if it is not set or not a boolean property. |
| <a id="api-92a6035df27b4644"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetCameraDriver(int index)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L27905) | Use this function to get the name of a built in camera driver. The list of camera drivers is given in the order that they are normally initialized by default; the drivers that seem more reasonable to choose first (as far as the SDL developers believe) are earlier in the list. The names of drivers are all simple, low-ASCII identifiers, like "v4l2", "coremedia" or "android". These never have Unicode characters, and are not meant to be proper names. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetNumCameraDrivers<br>`index`：the index of the camera driver; the value ranges from 0 to SDL_GetNumCameraDrivers() - 1.<br>返回：the name of the camera driver at the requested index, or NULL if an invalid index was specified. |
| <a id="api-b060c9bfcf80f1e7"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetCameraFormat(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLCamera* camera, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLCameraSpec* spec)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L27735) | Get the spec that a camera is using when generating images. Note that this might not be the native format of the hardware, as SDL might be converting to this format behind the scenes. If the system is waiting for the user to approve access to the camera, as some platforms require, this will return false, but this isn't necessarily a fatal error; you should either wait for an SDL_EVENT_CAMERA_DEVICE_APPROVED (or SDL_EVENT_CAMERA_DEVICE_DENIED) event, or poll SDL_GetCameraPermissionState() occasionally until it returns non-zero. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_OpenCamera<br>`camera`：opened camera device.<br>`spec`：the SDL_CameraSpec to be initialized by this function.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-05b6983dddde978f"></a>[`public uint Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetCameraId(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLCamera* camera)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L37405) | Get the instance ID of an opened camera. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_OpenCamera<br>`camera`：an SDL_Camera to query.<br>返回：the instance ID of the specified camera on success or 0 on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-8e8a25bd9b8201cc"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetCameraName(uint instance_id)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L40548) | Get the human-readable device name for a camera. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetCameras<br>`instance_id`：the camera device instance ID.<br>返回：a human-readable device name or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-713b99a65ef0ba48"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLCameraPermissionState Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetCameraPermissionState(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLCamera* camera)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L20176) | Query if camera access has been approved by the user. Cameras will not function between when the device is opened by the app and when the user permits access to the hardware. On some platforms, this presents as a popup dialog where the user has to explicitly approve access; on others the approval might be implicit and not alert the user at all. This function can be used to check the status of that approval. It will return SDL_CAMERA_PERMISSION_STATE_PENDING if waiting for user response, SDL_CAMERA_PERMISSION_STATE_APPROVED if the camera is approved for use, and SDL_CAMERA_PERMISSION_STATE_DENIED if the user denied access. Instead of polling with this function, you can wait for a SDL_EVENT_CAMERA_DEVICE_APPROVED (or SDL_EVENT_CAMERA_DEVICE_DENIED) event in the standard SDL event loop, which is guaranteed to be sent once when permission to use the camera is decided. If a camera is declined, there's nothing to be done but call SDL_CloseCamera() to dispose of it. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_OpenCamera @sa SDL_CloseCamera<br>`camera`：the opened camera device to query.<br>返回：an SDL_CameraPermissionState value indicating if access is granted, or `SDL_CAMERA_PERMISSION_STATE_PENDING` if the decision is still pending. |
| <a id="api-0ecf81cbe7c3fe36"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLCameraPosition Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetCameraPosition(uint instance_id)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L21227) | Get the position of the camera in relation to the system. Most platforms will report UNKNOWN, but mobile devices, like phones, can often make a distinction between cameras on the front of the device (that points towards the user, for taking "selfies") and cameras on the back (for filming in the direction the user is facing). @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetCameras<br>`instance_id`：the camera device instance ID.<br>返回：the position of the camera on the system hardware. |
| <a id="api-d5643fb6b5e7a708"></a>[`public uint Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetCameraProperties(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLCamera* camera)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L28751) | Get the properties associated with an opened camera. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`camera`：the SDL_Camera obtained from SDL_OpenCamera().<br>返回：a valid property ID on success or 0 on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-13d7f8a4327a5e4f"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLCameraSpec** Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetCameraSupportedFormats(uint instance_id, int* count)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L45622) | Get the list of native formats/sizes a camera supports. This returns a list of all formats and frame sizes that a specific camera can offer. This is useful if your app can accept a variety of image formats and sizes and so want to find the optimal spec that doesn't require conversion. This function isn't strictly required; if you call SDL_OpenCamera with a NULL spec, SDL will choose a native format for you, and if you instead specify a desired format, it will transparently convert to the requested format on your behalf. If `count` is not NULL, it will be filled with the number of elements in the returned array. Note that it's legal for a camera to supply an empty list. This is what will happen on Emscripten builds, since that platform won't tell _anything_ about available cameras until you've opened one, and won't even tell if there _is_ a camera until the user has given you permission to check through a scary warning popup. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetCameras @sa SDL_OpenCamera<br>`instance_id`：the camera device instance ID.<br>`count`：a pointer filled in with the number of elements in the list, may be NULL.<br>返回：a NULL terminated array of pointers to SDL_CameraSpec or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. This is a single allocation that should be freed with SDL_free() when it is no longer needed. |
| <a id="api-6acf1746770c44a2"></a>[`public uint* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetCameras(int* count)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L29376) | Get a list of currently connected camera devices. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_OpenCamera<br>`count`：a pointer filled in with the number of cameras returned, may be NULL.<br>返回：a 0 terminated array of camera instance IDs or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. This should be freed with SDL_free() when it is no longer needed. |
| <a id="api-9d501abcdf4d8ccb"></a>[`public void* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetClipboardData(sbyte* mime_type, ulong* size)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L42297) | Get the data from the clipboard for a given mime type. The size of text data does not include the terminator, but the text is guaranteed to be null-terminated. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_HasClipboardData @sa SDL_SetClipboardData<br>`mime_type`：the mime type to read from the clipboard.<br>`size`：a pointer filled in with the length of the returned data.<br>返回：the retrieved data buffer or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. This should be freed with SDL_free() when it is no longer needed. |
| <a id="api-c761ba3e1489f640"></a>[`public sbyte** Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetClipboardMimeTypes(ulong* num_mime_types)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L46461) | Retrieve the list of mime types available in the clipboard. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetClipboardData<br>`num_mime_types`：a pointer filled with the number of mime types, may be NULL.<br>返回：a null-terminated array of strings with mime types, or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. This should be freed with SDL_free() when it is no longer needed. |
| <a id="api-4b98d01f747ad5ef"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetClipboardText()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L40416) | Get UTF-8 text from the clipboard. This function returns an empty string if there is not enough memory left for a copy of the clipboard's content. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_HasClipboardText @sa SDL_SetClipboardText<br>返回：the clipboard text on success or an empty string on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. This should be freed with SDL_free() when it is no longer needed. |
| <a id="api-a2a23cfe4723b6ca"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetClosestFullscreenDisplayMode(uint displayID, int w, int h, float refresh_rate, byte include_high_density_modes, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLDisplayMode* closest)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L23842) | Get the closest match to the requested display mode. The available display modes are scanned and `closest` is filled in with the closest mode matching the requested mode and returned. The mode format and refresh rate default to the desktop mode if they are set to 0. The modes are scanned with size being first priority, format being second priority, and finally checking the refresh rate. If all the available modes are too small, then false is returned. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetDisplays @sa SDL_GetFullscreenDisplayModes<br>`displayID`：the instance ID of the display to query.<br>`w`：the width in pixels of the desired display mode.<br>`h`：the height in pixels of the desired display mode.<br>`refresh_rate`：the refresh rate of the desired display mode, or 0.0f for the desktop refresh rate.<br>`include_high_density_modes`：boolean to include high density modes in the search.<br>`closest`：a pointer filled in with the closest display mode equal to or larger than the desired mode.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-3d108b0eafc1689b"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetCpuCacheLineSize()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L52927) | Determine the L1 cache line size of the CPU. This is useful for determining multi-threaded structure padding or SIMD prefetch sizes. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>返回：the L1 cache line size of the CPU, in bytes. |
| <a id="api-aa4bf48a099ac6fb"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetCurrentAudioDriver()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L21870) | Get the name of the current audio driver. The names of drivers are all simple, low-ASCII identifiers, like "alsa", "coreaudio" or "wasapi". These never have Unicode characters, and are not meant to be proper names. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>返回：the name of the current audio driver or NULL if no driver has been initialized. |
| <a id="api-8ce533d272dd2325"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetCurrentCameraDriver()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L23999) | Get the name of the current camera driver. The names of drivers are all simple, low-ASCII identifiers, like "v4l2", "coremedia" or "android". These never have Unicode characters, and are not meant to be proper names. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>返回：the name of the current camera driver or NULL if no driver has been initialized. |
| <a id="api-1dd2b8009f16bda4"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetCurrentDirectory()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L21821) | Get what the system believes is the "current working directory." For systems without a concept of a current working directory, this will still attempt to provide something reasonable. SDL does not provide a means to _change_ the current working directory; for platforms without this concept, this would cause surprises with file access outside of SDL. The returned path is guaranteed to end with a path separator (' \ ' on Windows, '/' on most other platforms). @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>返回：a UTF-8 string of the current working directory in platform-dependent notation. NULL if there's a problem. This should be freed with SDL_free() when it is no longer needed. |
| <a id="api-dca0ff9f6fe4a71e"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLDisplayMode* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetCurrentDisplayMode(uint displayID)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L31973) | Get information about the current display mode. There's a difference between this function and SDL_GetDesktopDisplayMode() when SDL runs fullscreen and has changed the resolution. In that case this function will return the current display mode, and not the previous native display mode. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetDesktopDisplayMode @sa SDL_GetDisplays<br>`displayID`：the instance ID of the display to query.<br>返回：a pointer to the desktop display mode or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-960a11af49559596"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLDisplayOrientation Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetCurrentDisplayOrientation(uint displayID)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L33805) | Get the orientation of a display. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetDisplays<br>`displayID`：the instance ID of the display to query.<br>返回：the SDL_DisplayOrientation enum value of the display, or `SDL_ORIENTATION_UNKNOWN` if it isn't available. |
| <a id="api-6f372117b8c6d842"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetCurrentRenderOutputSize(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRenderer* renderer, int* w, int* h)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L34693) | Get the current output size in pixels of a rendering context. If a rendering target is active, this will return the size of the rendering target in pixels, otherwise return the value of SDL_GetRenderOutputSize(). Rendering target or not, the output will be adjusted by the current logical presentation state, dictated by SDL_SetRenderLogicalPresentation(). @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetRenderOutputSize<br>`renderer`：the rendering context.<br>`w`：a pointer filled in with the current width.<br>`h`：a pointer filled in with the current height.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-be3eb342fffd7729"></a>[`public ulong Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetCurrentThreadId()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L40241) | Get the thread identifier for the current thread. This thread identifier is as reported by the underlying operating system. If SDL is running on a platform that does not support threads the return value will always be zero. This function also returns a valid thread ID when called from the main thread. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetThreadID<br>返回：the ID of the current thread. |
| <a id="api-02129410db350729"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetCurrentTime(long* ticks)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L25595) | Gets the current value of the system realtime clock in nanoseconds since Jan 1, 1970 in Universal Coordinated Time (UTC). @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`ticks`：the SDL_Time to hold the returned tick count.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-432bbeeabbceee29"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetCurrentVideoDriver()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L19128) | Get the name of the currently initialized video driver. The names of drivers are all simple, low-ASCII identifiers, like "cocoa", "x11" or "windows". These never have Unicode characters, and are not meant to be proper names. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetNumVideoDrivers @sa SDL_GetVideoDriver<br>返回：the name of the current video driver or NULL if no driver has been initialized. |
| <a id="api-11dfe261fbb2f777"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLCursor* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetCursor()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L28264) | Get the active cursor. This function returns a pointer to the current cursor which is owned by the library. It is not necessary to free the cursor with SDL_DestroyCursor(). @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetCursor<br>返回：the active cursor or NULL if there is no mouse. |
| <a id="api-026fa7a082a54c41"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetDateTimeLocalePreferences(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLDateFormat* dateFormat, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLTimeFormat* timeFormat)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L26076) | Gets the current preferred date and time format for the system locale. This might be a "slow" call that has to query the operating system. It's best to ask for this once and save the results. However, the preferred formats can change, usually because the user has changed a system preference outside of your program. @threadsafety This function is not thread safe. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`dateFormat`：a pointer to the SDL_DateFormat to hold the returned date format, may be NULL.<br>`timeFormat`：a pointer to the SDL_TimeFormat to hold the returned time format, may be NULL.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-23b2f83852f31cb0"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetDayOfWeek(int year, int month, int day)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L24685) | Get the day of week for a calendar date. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`year`：the year component of the date.<br>`month`：the month component of the date.<br>`day`：the day component of the date.<br>返回：a value between 0 and 6 (0 being Sunday) if the date is valid or -1 on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-a8a6970e814b135f"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetDayOfYear(int year, int month, int day)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L41155) | Get the day of year for a calendar date. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`year`：the year component of the date.<br>`month`：the month component of the date.<br>`day`：the day component of the date.<br>返回：the day of year [0-365] if the date is valid or -1 on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-35875ed0b153c67d"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetDaysInMonth(int year, int month)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L18408) | Get the number of days in a month for a given year. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`year`：the year.<br>`month`：the month [1-12].<br>返回：the number of days in the requested month or -1 on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-03b58b4a04c689a2"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLCursor* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetDefaultCursor()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L36985) | Get the default cursor. You do not have to call SDL_DestroyCursor() on the return value, but it is safe to do so. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>返回：the default cursor on success or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-410c998235fe2fe7"></a>[`public delegate* unmanaged[Cdecl]<void*, int, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLLogPriority, sbyte*, void> Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetDefaultLogOutputFunction()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L41209) | Get the default log output function. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetLogOutputFunction @sa SDL_GetLogOutputFunction<br>返回：the default log output callback. It should be called with NULL for the userdata argument. |
| <a id="api-b84d1e4c514db00d"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetDefaultTextureScaleMode(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRenderer* renderer, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLScaleMode* scale_mode)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L31892) | Get default texture scale mode of the given renderer. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.4.0. @sa SDL_SetDefaultTextureScaleMode<br>`renderer`：the renderer to get data from.<br>`scale_mode`：a SDL_ScaleMode filled with current default scale mode. See SDL_SetDefaultTextureScaleMode() for the meaning of the value.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-5c7c0aa4291027a4"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLDisplayMode* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetDesktopDisplayMode(uint displayID)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L42351) | Get information about the desktop's display mode. There's a difference between this function and SDL_GetCurrentDisplayMode() when SDL runs fullscreen and has changed the resolution. In that case this function will return the previous native display mode, and not the current display mode. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetCurrentDisplayMode @sa SDL_GetDisplays<br>`displayID`：the instance ID of the display to query.<br>返回：a pointer to the desktop display mode or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-3aaf6124765f4686"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetDirect3D9AdapterIndex(uint displayID)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L42062) | Get the D3D9 adapter index that matches the specified display. The returned adapter index can be passed to `IDirect3D9::CreateDevice` and controls on which monitor a full screen application will appear. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`displayID`：the instance of the display to query.<br>返回：the D3D9 adapter index on success or -1 on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-88408973383a8ab7"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetDisplayBounds(uint displayID, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRect* rect)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L38360) | Get the desktop area represented by a display. The primary display is often located at (0,0), but may be placed at a different location depending on monitor layout. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetDisplayUsableBounds @sa SDL_GetDisplays<br>`displayID`：the instance ID of the display to query.<br>`rect`：the SDL_Rect structure filled in with the display bounds.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-716256e5c22e1b4e"></a>[`public float Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetDisplayContentScale(uint displayID)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L28913) | Get the content scale of a display. The content scale is the expected scale for content based on the DPI settings of the display. For example, a 4K display might have a 2.0 (200%) display scale, which means that the user expects UI elements to be twice as big on this display, to aid in readability. After window creation, SDL_GetWindowDisplayScale() should be used to query the content scale factor for individual windows instead of querying the display for a window and calling this function, as the per-window content scale factor may differ from the base value of the display it is on, particularly on high-DPI and/or multi-monitor desktop configurations. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetWindowDisplayScale @sa SDL_GetDisplays<br>`displayID`：the instance ID of the display to query.<br>返回：the content scale of the display, or 0.0f on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-3a98b3a03ea3aadd"></a>[`public uint Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetDisplayForPoint(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLPoint* point)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L44550) | Get the display containing a point. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetDisplayBounds @sa SDL_GetDisplays<br>`point`：the point to query.<br>返回：the instance ID of the display containing the point or 0 on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-8509e960a7bd7a6e"></a>[`public uint Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetDisplayForRect(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRect* rect)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L39397) | Get the display primarily containing a rect. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetDisplayBounds @sa SDL_GetDisplays<br>`rect`：the rect to query.<br>返回：the instance ID of the display entirely containing the rect or closest to the center of the rect on success or 0 on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-57e23a0750787bed"></a>[`public uint Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetDisplayForWindow(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L26735) | Get the display associated with a window. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetDisplayBounds @sa SDL_GetDisplays<br>`window`：the window to query.<br>返回：the instance ID of the display containing the center of the window on success or 0 on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-fedb049ca3693e9a"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetDisplayName(uint displayID)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L17118) | Get the name of a display in UTF-8 encoding. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetDisplays<br>`displayID`：the instance ID of the display to query.<br>返回：the name of a display or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-552885809636ec65"></a>[`public uint Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetDisplayProperties(uint displayID)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L31718) | Get the properties associated with a display. The following read-only properties are provided by SDL: - `SDL_PROP_DISPLAY_HDR_ENABLED_BOOLEAN`: true if the display has HDR headroom above the SDR white point. This is for informational and diagnostic purposes only, as not all platforms provide this information at the display level. On KMS/DRM: - `SDL_PROP_DISPLAY_KMSDRM_PANEL_ORIENTATION_NUMBER`: the "panel orientation" property for the display in degrees of clockwise rotation. Note that this is provided only as a hint, and the application is responsible for any coordinate transformations needed to conform to the requested display orientation. On Wayland: - `SDL_PROP_DISPLAY_WAYLAND_WL_OUTPUT_POINTER`: the wl_output associated with the display On Windows: - `SDL_PROP_DISPLAY_WINDOWS_HMONITOR_POINTER`: the monitor handle (HMONITOR) associated with the display @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`displayID`：the instance ID of the display to query.<br>返回：a valid property ID on success or 0 on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-da01c08e9b542c0e"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetDisplayUsableBounds(uint displayID, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRect* rect)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L33837) | Get the usable desktop area represented by a display, in screen coordinates. This is the same area as SDL_GetDisplayBounds() reports, but with portions reserved by the system removed. For example, on Apple's macOS, this subtracts the area occupied by the menu bar and dock.
