Setting a window to be fullscreen generally bypasses these unusable areas, so these are good guidelines for the maximum space available to a non-fullscreen window. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetDisplayBounds @sa SDL_GetDisplays<br>`displayID`：the instance ID of the display to query.<br>`rect`：the SDL_Rect structure filled in with the display bounds.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-5f86920cf16cc286"></a>[`public uint* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetDisplays(int* count)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L28848) | Get a list of currently connected displays. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`count`：a pointer filled in with the number of displays returned, may be NULL.<br>返回：a 0 terminated array of display instance IDs or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. This should be freed with SDL_free() when it is no longer needed. |
| <a id="api-a18a9ae4ecdbdb33"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetDxgiOutputInfo(uint displayID, int* adapterIndex, int* outputIndex)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L22179) | Get the DXGI Adapter and Output indices for the specified display. The DXGI Adapter and Output indices can be passed to `EnumAdapters` and `EnumOutputs` respectively to get the objects required to create a DX10 or DX11 device and swap chain. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`displayID`：the instance of the display to query.<br>`adapterIndex`：a pointer to be filled in with the adapter index.<br>`outputIndex`：a pointer to be filled in with the output index.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-1fddf6dd1c607bc1"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLEnvironment* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetEnvironment()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L17097) | Get the process environment. This is initialized at application start and is not affected by setenv() and unsetenv() calls after that point. Use SDL_SetEnvironmentVariable() and SDL_UnsetEnvironmentVariable() if you want to modify this environment, or SDL_setenv_unsafe() or SDL_unsetenv_unsafe() if you want changes to persist in the C runtime environment after SDL_Quit(). @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetEnvironmentVariable @sa SDL_GetEnvironmentVariables @sa SDL_SetEnvironmentVariable @sa SDL_UnsetEnvironmentVariable<br>返回：a pointer to the environment for the process or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-d079df5e18eac1e2"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetEnvironmentVariable(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLEnvironment* env, sbyte* name)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L40844) | Get the value of a variable in the environment. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetEnvironment @sa SDL_CreateEnvironment @sa SDL_GetEnvironmentVariables @sa SDL_SetEnvironmentVariable @sa SDL_UnsetEnvironmentVariable<br>`env`：the environment to query.<br>`name`：the name of the variable to get.<br>返回：a pointer to the value of the variable or NULL if it can't be found. |
| <a id="api-2de69f145e0623ec"></a>[`public sbyte** Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetEnvironmentVariables(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLEnvironment* env)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L29787) | Get all variables in the environment. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetEnvironment @sa SDL_CreateEnvironment @sa SDL_GetEnvironmentVariable @sa SDL_SetEnvironmentVariable @sa SDL_UnsetEnvironmentVariable<br>`env`：the environment to query.<br>返回：a NULL terminated array of pointers to environment variables in the form "variable=value" or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. This is a single allocation that should be freed with SDL_free() when it is no longer needed. |
| <a id="api-c86dc24e5e8c0c75"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetError()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L29957) | Retrieve a message about the last error that occurred on the current thread. It is possible for multiple errors to occur before calling SDL_GetError(). Only the last error is returned. The message is only applicable when an SDL function has signaled an error. You must check the return values of SDL function calls to determine when to appropriately call SDL_GetError(). You should *not* use the results of SDL_GetError() to decide if an error has occurred! Sometimes SDL will set an error string even when reporting success. SDL will *not* clear the error string for successful API calls. You *must* check return values for failure cases before you can assume the error string applies. Error strings are set per-thread, so an error set in a different thread will not interfere with the current thread's operation. The returned value is a thread-local string which will remain valid until the current thread's error string is changed. The caller should make a copy if the value is needed after the next SDL API call. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_ClearError @sa SDL_SetError<br>返回：a message with information about the specific error that occurred, or an empty string if there hasn't been an error message set since the last call to SDL_ClearError(). |
| <a id="api-5e11eda2bc603d79"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetEventDescription(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLEvent* @event, sbyte* buf, int buflen)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L34469) | Generate an English description of an event. This will fill `buf` with a null-terminated string that might look something like this: ``` SDL_EVENT_MOUSE_MOTION (timestamp=1140256324 windowid=2 which=0 state=0 x=492.99 y=139.09 xrel=52 yrel=6) ``` The exact format of the string is not guaranteed; it is intended for logging purposes, to be read by a human, and not parsed by a computer. The returned value follows the same rules as SDL_snprintf(): `buf` will always be NULL-terminated (unless `buflen` is zero), and will be truncated if `buflen` is too small. The return code is the number of bytes needed for the complete string, not counting the NULL-terminator, whether the string was truncated or not. Unlike SDL_snprintf(), though, this function never returns -1. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.4.0.<br>`event`：an event to describe. May be NULL.<br>`buf`：the buffer to fill with the description string. May be NULL.<br>`buflen`：the maximum bytes that can be written to `buf`.<br>返回：number of bytes needed for the full string, not counting the null-terminator byte. |
| <a id="api-2eb6551521d4474d"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetEventFilter(delegate* unmanaged[Cdecl]<void*, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLEvent*, byte>* filter, void** userdata)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L35148) | Query the current event filter. This function can be used to "chain" filters, by saving the existing filter before replacing it with a function that will call that saved filter. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetEventFilter<br>`filter`：the current callback function will be stored here.<br>`userdata`：the pointer that is passed to the current event filter will be stored here.<br>返回：true on success or false if there is no event filter set. |
| <a id="api-d34618cf2367786b"></a>[`public float Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetFloatProperty(uint props, sbyte* name, float default_value)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L46939) | Get a floating point property from a group of properties. You can use SDL_GetPropertyType() to query whether the property exists and is a floating point property. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetPropertyType @sa SDL_HasProperty @sa SDL_SetFloatProperty<br>`props`：the properties to query.<br>`name`：the name of the property to query.<br>`default_value`：the default value of the property.<br>返回：the value of the property, or `default_value` if it is not set or not a float property. |
| <a id="api-704c2132e4ef7a3e"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLDisplayMode** Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetFullscreenDisplayModes(uint displayID, int* count)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L26547) | Get a list of fullscreen display modes available on a display. The display modes are sorted in this priority: - w -> largest to smallest - h -> largest to smallest - bits per pixel -> more colors to fewer colors - packed pixel layout -> largest to smallest - refresh rate -> highest to lowest - pixel density -> lowest to highest @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetDisplays<br>`displayID`：the instance ID of the display to query.<br>`count`：a pointer filled in with the number of display modes returned, may be NULL.<br>返回：a NULL terminated array of display mode pointers or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. This is a single allocation that should be freed with SDL_free() when it is no longer needed. |
| <a id="api-882efcfd901ae7e7"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetGamepadAppleSfSymbolsNameForAxis(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGamepad* gamepad, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGamepadAxis axis)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L40061) | Return the sfSymbolsName for a given axis on a gamepad on Apple platforms. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetGamepadAppleSFSymbolsNameForButton<br>`gamepad`：the gamepad to query.<br>`axis`：an axis on the gamepad.<br>返回：the sfSymbolsName or NULL if the name can't be found. |
| <a id="api-39b7efa4baa37e5f"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetGamepadAppleSfSymbolsNameForButton(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGamepad* gamepad, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGamepadButton button)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L21578) | Return the sfSymbolsName for a given button on a gamepad on Apple platforms. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetGamepadAppleSFSymbolsNameForAxis<br>`gamepad`：the gamepad to query.<br>`button`：a button on the gamepad.<br>返回：the sfSymbolsName or NULL if the name can't be found. |
| <a id="api-24e0336fea1589b3"></a>[`public short Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetGamepadAxis(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGamepad* gamepad, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGamepadAxis axis)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L23351) | Get the current state of an axis control on a gamepad. The axis indices start at index 0. For thumbsticks, the state is a value ranging from -32768 (up/left) to 32767 (down/right). Triggers range from 0 when released to 32767 when fully pressed, and never return a negative value. Note that this differs from the value reported by the lower-level SDL_GetJoystickAxis(), which normally uses the full range. Note that for invalid gamepads or axes, this will return 0. Zero is also a valid value in normal operation; usually it means a centered axis. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GamepadHasAxis @sa SDL_GetGamepadButton<br>`gamepad`：a gamepad.<br>`axis`：an axis index (one of the SDL_GamepadAxis values).<br>返回：axis state. |
| <a id="api-ab6865d730259bf0"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGamepadAxis Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetGamepadAxisFromString(sbyte* str)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L46155) | Convert a string into SDL_GamepadAxis enum. This function is called internally to translate SDL_Gamepad mapping strings for the underlying joystick device into the consistent SDL_Gamepad mapping. You do not normally need to call this function unless you are parsing SDL_Gamepad mappings in your own code. Note specially that "righttrigger" and "lefttrigger" map to `SDL_GAMEPAD_AXIS_RIGHT_TRIGGER` and `SDL_GAMEPAD_AXIS_LEFT_TRIGGER`, respectively. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetGamepadStringForAxis<br>`str`：string representing a SDL_Gamepad axis.<br>返回：the SDL_GamepadAxis enum corresponding to the input string, or `SDL_GAMEPAD_AXIS_INVALID` if no match was found. |
| <a id="api-4943572742b7f333"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGamepadBinding** Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetGamepadBindings(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGamepad* gamepad, int* count)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L32184) | Get the SDL joystick layer bindings for a gamepad. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`gamepad`：a gamepad.<br>`count`：a pointer filled in with the number of bindings returned.<br>返回：a NULL terminated array of pointers to bindings or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. This is a single allocation that should be freed with SDL_free() when it is no longer needed. |
| <a id="api-37164e1f361d189d"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetGamepadButton(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGamepad* gamepad, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGamepadButton button)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L40474) | Get the current state of a button on a gamepad. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GamepadHasButton @sa SDL_GetGamepadAxis<br>`gamepad`：a gamepad.<br>`button`：a button index (one of the SDL_GamepadButton values).<br>返回：true if the button is pressed, false otherwise. |
| <a id="api-45e3db62d0b9151d"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGamepadButton Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetGamepadButtonFromString(sbyte* str)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L24024) | Convert a string into an SDL_GamepadButton enum. This function is called internally to translate SDL_Gamepad mapping strings for the underlying joystick device into the consistent SDL_Gamepad mapping. You do not normally need to call this function unless you are parsing SDL_Gamepad mappings in your own code. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetGamepadStringForButton<br>`str`：string representing a SDL_Gamepad button.<br>返回：the SDL_GamepadButton enum corresponding to the input string, or `SDL_GAMEPAD_BUTTON_INVALID` if no match was found. |
| <a id="api-0e47bd0e6e48ed48"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGamepadButtonLabel Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetGamepadButtonLabel(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGamepad* gamepad, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGamepadButton button)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L24529) | Get the label of a button on a gamepad. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetGamepadButtonLabelForType<br>`gamepad`：a gamepad.<br>`button`：a button index (one of the SDL_GamepadButton values).<br>返回：the SDL_GamepadButtonLabel enum corresponding to the button label. |
| <a id="api-f3e259960ecf9e43"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGamepadButtonLabel Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetGamepadButtonLabelForType(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGamepadType type, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGamepadButton button)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L32559) | Get the label of a button on a gamepad. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetGamepadButtonLabel<br>`type`：the type of gamepad to check.<br>`button`：a button index (one of the SDL_GamepadButton values).<br>返回：the SDL_GamepadButtonLabel enum corresponding to the button label. |
| <a id="api-b7740da2f36c865c"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLJoystickConnectionState Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetGamepadConnectionState(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGamepad* gamepad)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L40982) | Get the connection state of a gamepad. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`gamepad`：the gamepad object to query.<br>返回：the connection state on success or `SDL_JOYSTICK_CONNECTION_INVALID` on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-55f0d0089721d97d"></a>[`public ushort Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetGamepadFirmwareVersion(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGamepad* gamepad)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L31004) | Get the firmware version of an opened gamepad, if available. If the firmware version isn't available this function returns 0. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`gamepad`：the gamepad object to query.<br>返回：the gamepad firmware version, or zero if unavailable. |
| <a id="api-999d04ebf4d7aa08"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGamepad* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetGamepadFromId(uint instance_id)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L38333) | Get the SDL_Gamepad associated with a joystick instance ID, if it has been opened. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`instance_id`：the joystick instance ID of the gamepad.<br>返回：an SDL_Gamepad on success or NULL on failure or if it hasn't been opened yet; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-3448d06a62c4c577"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGamepad* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetGamepadFromPlayerIndex(int player_index)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L30079) | Get the SDL_Gamepad associated with a player index. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetGamepadPlayerIndex @sa SDL_SetGamepadPlayerIndex<br>`player_index`：the player index, which different from the instance ID.<br>返回：the SDL_Gamepad associated with a player index. |
| <a id="api-7790a6cd64d0a98c"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetGamepadGuidForId(uint instance_id, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGUID* bgcs_result)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L17218) | Get the implementation-dependent GUID of a gamepad. This can be called before any gamepads are opened. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GUIDToString @sa SDL_GetGamepads<br>`instance_id`：the joystick instance ID.<br>`bgcs_result`：Native parameter.<br>约束：Native result: the GUID of the selected gamepad. If called on an invalid index, this function returns a zero GUID. |
| <a id="api-dd873181e31cc403"></a>[`public uint Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetGamepadId(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGamepad* gamepad)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L24214) | Get the instance ID of an opened gamepad. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`gamepad`：a gamepad identifier previously returned by SDL_OpenGamepad().<br>返回：the instance ID of the specified gamepad on success or 0 on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-ee796e4561b50b3c"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLJoystick* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.GetGamepadJoystick(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGamepad* gamepad)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L50880) | Get the underlying joystick from a gamepad. This function will give you a SDL_Joystick object, which allows you to use the SDL_Joystick functions with a SDL_Gamepad object. This would be useful for getting a joystick's position at any given time, even if it hasn't moved (moving it would produce an event, which would have the axis' value). The pointer returned is owned by the SDL_Gamepad. You should not call SDL_CloseJoystick() on it, for example, since doing so will likely cause SDL to crash. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`gamepad`：the gamepad object that you want to get a joystick from.<br>返回：an SDL_Joystick object, or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
